# Rendering cvar and console-command provenance

This audit accompanies [rendering features](rendering-features.md). It compares
currently registered engine/game/renderer names in this source snapshot against
the **local** `rend2` branch. Names are case-insensitive and deduplicated across
SP, MP and shared code. A name inherited anywhere in the baseline is excluded,
even if this branch adds another renderer or registration site.

- Baseline: local `rend2` at [`1a6a64342`](https://github.com/keyten/BetterJK/commit/1a6a643427aa347553e9073dac5570b33337c4d9).
- Audited source: `rend2-unified-wip` at [`df7da6eb1`](https://github.com/keyten/BetterJK/commit/df7da6eb1a9f98c8bd22a57a7e21a2c712fb81d3).
- `origin/rend2` was [`1215e136c`](https://github.com/keyten/BetterJK/commit/1215e136cf378b2b3ec2e10821221c3b6692b473); it is a different ref and was not substituted for the requested baseline.
- Result: **687 cvars and 55 console commands** absent from the baseline and registered in the audited source.
- Original feature-page coverage: **348 cvars and 30 commands missing**, including four misspelled particle-lighting names. The updated page mentions all 742 current names.

## Method and interpretation

The inventory scans tracked `code/` (SP), `codemp/` (MP),
`shared/` and `codeJK2/` source registrations: `Cvar_Get` and its
`ri_Cvar_Get_NoComm` wrapper, SP game-import `gi.cvar`,
`Cvar_Register`, VM cvar tables,
`XCVAR_DEF`, the shared `LIGHTRECON_CONTROL/CONSUMER` list,
literal console-command registrations and `consoleCommand_t` tables.
Lazy getters outside `tr_init.cpp` are included. Comments, removed names,
UI script-language actions and plain reads/declarations are excluded.

For each new name, the first added registration in `rend2..HEAD` was found
in the patch history. The exact line at that commit was then checked with
`git blame --line-porcelain -L N,N <commit> -- <path>`. All **742**
introduction checks returned that introducing commit. Current registrations
were independently checked with `git blame -w -M -C`. **54** primary
registration lines now blame a later commit; the tables retain both results.

Examples for reproducing an individual check (replace the bracketed fields
with an introduction row's source location and commit):

~~~text
git diff rend2 <audited-source> -- code codemp shared codeJK2
git log --reverse -p rend2..<audited-source> -- <registration-file>
git blame --line-porcelain -L <line>,<line> <introducing-commit> -- <registration-file>
git blame -w -M -C --line-porcelain <audited-source> -- <current-registration-file>
~~~

The introducing commit means **first registration of the current name in this
branch difference**. A rename introduces the new spelling there; the underlying
feature can be older. Defaults and flags are from the audited source, not from
the introducing commit. Changing a default is not treated as a new cvar.
Controls removed before the audited snapshot are not advertised as available.

In the cvar tables, `ARCHIVE` saves the value, `LATCH` requires renderer
restart, `CHEAT` restricts changes and `ROM` denotes internal read-only
state. `CVAR_NONE` and numeric 0 are equivalent. Feature dependencies and
map-load policies still apply in addition to these flags. Engine-owned
`cl_renderer*` flags have ROM engine registrations and unflagged SP VM
mirrors; the table reports the engine registration.

“Current registration” links every distinct current source file, SP first when
present. “Current blame” is for that first listed site; it shows `same` when
it matches the introduction. All source links are pinned to the audited or
introducing commit so later line shifts do not change the evidence.

## Corrections to the feature reference

- Added missing water/liquid, atmosphere/cloud, physical-camera, PBR/BRDF,
  entity lighting, legacy-FX, rain/wetness, loading and optimization controls,
  plus the canonical reconstruction settings and observation diagnostics.
- Corrected `r_particleLightingingMix/Scale/Floor/Debug` to the registered
  `r_particleLightingMix/Scale/Floor/Debug` spellings.
- Replaced the removed directional-grid reconstruction description and removed
  `r_vfogStaticStats` command with the current baked-grid/sun-separation
  behavior. The [removal report](rend2-grid-reconstruction-removal.md) documents
  that history.
- Expanded puddle ripple modes from on/off to 0 off, 1 procedural, 2 real rain
  impacts and 3 hybrid.
- Clarified that authored particle media and optional automatic legacy-FX media
  coexist, and that canonical reconstruction has no production solver or
  rendering adapters at this snapshot.
